# Hardcoded operational constants inventory

Generated by `bun scripts/inventory-hardcoded-values.ts`. Re-run from the repository root; do not edit the tables by hand.

Scope: module-level numeric and duration constants, plus inline numeric thresholds, in `src/` and executable TypeScript, JavaScript, Python and shell files under `scripts/`. The generator reports candidates mechanically; `scripts/inventory-hardcoded-values.classifications.json` records the code-reviewed decision for every item and any missing key fails the run. `operational` items are candidates for the later area PRs. Safety floors and contracts stay fixed; test seams remain injectable.
